rimless is more hygienic; the frame is chosen TO SUIT the pan with 18 cm depth as the safe project allowance and compatibility set by the fixing studs; and THE SEQUENCING FACT — the frame mounts on the CONCRETE floor before screed and tiling, so the WC type is a PLANNING-stage decision.** **⚠️ And a coupling: a tile-insert flush button requires the installation to be faced IN TILE — clad it in MDF or HPL and you must use a surface-mounted button.** **⚠️ Hygienic shower: one-point vs two-point is a PLANNING decision because two-point needs more wall space — her worked case has 850 where 900 would be better, so one-point.** **⚠️ Washer/dryer: built-in cabinet ~600 × 600, free-standing ~700; a dryer stacks on a washer via a SPECIAL MOUNTING KIT, not through a chipboard shelf.** **⚠️ Storage, on which she is emphatic — "можно офигенно круто выбрать смесители, но не предусмотреть место для хранения, и это всегда будет провально"** — cupboard above the installation by default at 400 mm projection, storage behind the mirror where short, and a reach check (800–1700 is beyond a short person). **⚠️ ACCESS FOR SERVICING, a genuine gap in this vault: provide trap access by hatch, removable tile or through a cabinet — she pre-empts the modern-traps-clean-from-outside objection with "случается разное"; DRAW THE HATCH ON THE VISUALISATION so the client understands there will be a seam; a free-standing bath cannot have one; and locate meters at survey.** **⚠️ Closes with a "красиво но нереализуемо" gallery reading a dozen real renderings** — free-standing baths that cannot be cleaned behind, podiums too narrow to stand on safely, mirrors too high to wash, timber vanities in wet rooms, screens with no room to open — **and the client-management rule: warn them about basin height, "если его это устроит, он подпишется на это."** [[_Sources/YT_yqb7N1yA9hg_mikhailovskaya_wc_basin_mixers_storage|note]].

- `yt_2h0XuwtM_4E` — «Обои в интерьере — устарело или нет?» (2023-07-17, Татьяна Михайловская) — **FULL (fact_yield 21, net 19), `promotional_ratio: low`. The unverified fourth item from the playlist triage shortlist, now processed — and narrower than the bathroom pair, as flagged: essentially one strong organising idea plus pattern rules and three justifications.** Her position is neither advocacy nor refusal: *"я использую обои в своих проектах, но очень дозировано и правильно."* **⚠️ ITS MOST VALUABLE CONTRIBUTION IS AN UNRESOLVED CONTRADICTION IT CREATES: she gives "cracks do not show through wallpaper" as the FIRST of her three justifications for specifying it, and this vault already records Мария Шеврина stating flatly the opposite — "wallpaper does not hide cracks, it accentuates them," with the mechanism (the crack telegraphs and the eye is drawn to it) and with reinforcing mesh dismissed as "пластырь на дыру в асфальте." Recorded as a Perspectives block rather than folded into either side, because it is a PHYSICAL, CHECKABLE claim — unusual in this vault — and because the case for wallpaper over paint in a new build rests substantially on it. Шеврина's side is currently better supported; it should be settled by a third source, not by weighing assertions.** **⚠️ The organising idea: wallpaper must not imitate what it cannot imitate, and the mechanism is TACTILE — brick, stone, tile, board and textured concrete are hard relief materials you know by touch, wallpaper is smooth, and "идёт диссонанс восприятия того, что мы видим, и того, что мы чувствуем." Plus THE RENDER TRAP, which explains why it keeps being specified: "на визуализации может смотреться хорошо, в интерьере никогда" — the failure is invisible in exactly the medium the decision is made in. Gloss compounds it.** **⚠️ Her test, which is the transferable part: ask the wallpaper "что вы имитируете?" and take it only if the answer matches what you see and feel** — worked across five samples, accepting a stamped-gold paint imitation and a painted fish panel, rejecting board, hard weave and stone. **Three legitimate justifications, all non-visual: technical/budget (with a real ALLOCATION argument — save on walls to fund furniture); style (Art Deco, classic, contemporary classic, Provence — where silk or fabric coverings are characteristic elements OF the style, and it need not be cheap); and function — ⚠️ the children's-room case, where the material's weakness is the argument for it: paper wallpaper is ecological, carries atmosphere, and is easily damaged and easily replaced, with the caveat that this applies to PAPER and NOT to non-woven.** **⚠️ Pattern: the repeat should be as large as possible, and the checkable test is that a pattern repeating twice within one roll width reads as a template; wider rolls are better for seams, but a narrow roll demands a larger repeat, and the two compound.** Plus **a precise composition constraint: unmounted art merges into a patterned wall — a picture works there only because of its white паспарту.** [[_Sources/YT_2h0XuwtM_4E_mikhailovskaya_wallpaper_selection|note]].

### ВАШ ДИЗАЙНЕР ИНТЕРЬЕРА / NSDSGN — a new channel, Round 1 trial, 6 of 220 videos (2026-09-03)

**⚠️ Channel context, because it decides how to read all six.** St Petersburg **premium design-*build* studio** (NSDSGN, `nsdsgn.ru`, Александр Синчуков) — it sells the renovation, not only the drawings, so the advertising filter applies at full strength and the description names the steering risk itself: «премиум класса». **Group B entry #4 in the channel queue, value previously unconfirmed. Full triage and round verdict in `_Inbox/planning/yourinteriordes_channel_triage_20260903.md`.**

**⚠️ Two channel-specific traps recorded there, both worth carrying to other channels.** (1) **This channel serves auto-translated metadata** — `yt-dlp` returned English titles until `--extractor-args youtube:lang=ru` was forced, which is the first *observed* instance of the standing-rule-1 trap in this vault rather than a guarded-against one; all six transcripts were fetched with `--languages ru` and confirmed genuinely Russian (2 manual tracks, 4 Russian ASR, none translated). (2) **View counts are noise here** — an 81-minute expert Q&A at 14 views against a 4-minute Ferrari vlog at 659 — so they must not be used to rank.

**⚠️ And the finding that decided the round: the content is carried by named guests, not the host.** Four of the six are guest formats, and the two highest-yield sources are a contractor and two lighting specialists. **Twice in six videos the host publishes expert testimony that limits his own professional authority** — a space organiser saying a designer «вряд ли мог» have foreseen storage, and two lighting specialists describing the designer as a generalist who must refer out, which he endorses. That is behavioural evidence about the channel, not tone.

- `yt_M0Wv4SOSUhs` — «15 важных вопросов о ремонте и дизайне. Ответы опытного строителя» (2023-12-15) — **FULL (fact_yield 34), `promotional_ratio: low`, `corroborates_existing: true`. The round's strongest source, and the content is the guest's**: Руслан, 30+ years in construction, ~20 in business-plus/премиум отделка and **старый фонд** reconstruction. Region **level 1** (SPb and Moscow both spoken and contrasted). **⚠️ The first St Petersburg per-m² tier ladder in this vault, Dec 2023 at 92.8875 RUB/USD**: floor **~10,000 (≈$110/m²)**, good brigade **~20,000 (≈$220)**, his stated minimum average **20,000–30,000 (≈$220–$320)**, premium **~70,000 (≈$750)**, **Moscow 90,000/100,000/150,000 (≈$970/≈$1,100/≈$1,600)** — scoped by him to construction work only, excluding classical work, decorative plaster, ventilation and balcony insulation, and flagged «не оферта». **⚠️ The 20,000 tier carries a free competence test better than the number: brigades there «говорят, что знают» about a теневой профиль or дверь скрытого монтажа and then say «а нафиг вам надо, давайте поменяем» — the substitution proposal is the diagnostic.** **⚠️ And the single most useful item is a mechanism for why cheap anecdotal per-m² figures circulate and are sincerely reported: a beginner with no price list copies a смета off a website of unknown age, finishes at a loss, and truthfully says «я сделал ремонт за 7.000» — true for him, false as a market rate.** Plus: the **cheapest quote's built-in abandonment arithmetic** (500,000 job, 200,000 advance spent, three weeks unpaid, «ключи в почтовом ящике»); **смета granularity tested on a plumbing line** (коллекторный узел with/without leak sensors and filters, тройниковая vs лучевая); **doors excluded and left to the manufacturer's fitters, because unpacking transfers the burden of proof**; **10% normal drift and a 10–15% buffer, caused physically by rough-stage measurements against post-plaster wall thickness**; **an OSB sheet at 800→1,600→2,400→3,600 RUB in ~15 days, year unstated so left unconverted, answered by substituting plasterboard — the requirement was the function, not the material**; **генподряд defined, with a ~300 m²/two-storey threshold below which it is pointless** (this project is far below it); **client-supplied subcontractors as a competence-in-context risk, observed cutting a duct on the kitchen island at final finish**; **«лучше не купить шкаф, чем сэкономить на автоматах в электрощите и проводке»**; cost × durability; **~1 year for 100 m²+, attributed to SUPPLY rather than curing — a different bottleneck from this vault's three existing sources**; and **авторский надзор justified by decision-making (the 250 mm shelf sized to the client's shampoo bottles) with its own failure mode named — supervision spent teaching the builder.** [[_Sources/YT_M0Wv4SOSUhs_nsdsgn_builder_qa_costs_and_estimates|note]].

- `yt_ZKAcnh4kKfY` — «10 вопросов о СВЕТЕ В ИНТЕРЬЕРЕ» (2024-01-09) — **FULL (fact_yield 27), `promotional_ratio: low`, `corroborates_existing: false`. Manual Russian caption track.** Guests Константин Цепелев (supplier, practising since 2006) and Матвей Борисов (lighting studio). **⚠️ Read the scope limit first: across 81 minutes there is no CRI figure, no lumen value, no wattage and no pulsation coefficient — it is a relationship podcast containing four excellent technical passages, not a technical podcast.** **⚠️ The headline item fills a real vault gap — `colour temperature` returned ZERO matches and `Kelvin` only six before this source: the Kruithof curve, and it unifies rules this vault held separately. «The brighter the light, the cooler it should be; the more dimmed, the warmer»**, stated against office norms (500–1000 lux → cooler; 200/300/150/100 lux → warmer). **Consequence: 4000 K belongs over a worktop because the zone is light-INTENSIVE, not because it is a kitchen** — which makes the vault's existing convention derivable rather than conventional. Plus a **height rule** (higher = cooler, closer to the eyes = warmer) and a **latitude rationale** for the warm residential default. **⚠️ Power supplies, the most checkable rule: sealed IP-rated metal units (his own running 10 years) versus the perforated/mesh and especially fanned units, which fail reliably in ~1–1.5 years against a 1-year warranty — a third independent channel for the overheating mechanism.** **⚠️ Dimming named as the single largest failure point, with the retrofit trap: an ordinary driver plus a dimmer added later produces strong flicker, so dimmability must be specified from the start.** **⚠️ Secondary optics given content — lenses at 10°/20°/30°/60°, the beam angle demanded as a written specification, and cheap optics shown to DEGRADE the CRI you paid for**, linking two parameters the vault held apart. **⚠️ Integrated-LED chandeliers shown to be structurally disposable via a service case: a bespoke L-shaped driver made only by that chandelier's maker, discontinued as unsuccessful — worst when the fixture was architecturally fitted to a niche; the rule is replaceability, not brand.** Plus **the replica ladder — one scraped photograph behind 180,000→15,000 RUB (≈$1,900→≈$160), and buying direct removes the supplier's отбраковка step**; a **~350,000 RUB (≈$3,700) super-economy euro-2/3-room figure that BUNDLES light and electrics and so is not a lighting-only benchmark**; **an honest refusal to invent a lighting-share-of-budget percentage**; a **Maslow bottom-up rule against marketing-driven voice control before base visibility**; and **the countertop installed before the question was asked — seconds to fit, a month to rework.** [[_Sources/YT_ZKAcnh4kKfY_nsdsgn_lighting_colour_temperature_and_gear|note]].

- `yt_9ZwqMKiMgvc` — «Как хранить вещи в МАЛЕНЬКОЙ квартире с БОЛЬШОЙ семьей» (2026-03-25) — **FULL (fact_yield 33), `promotional_ratio: low` — the lowest of the round, and nothing is being sold.** A walkthrough of professional организатор пространства Ксения's own flat (husband, 8-year-old, dog), renovated ~8 years earlier **with a design project** which she then largely rebuilt. **⚠️ The claim that matters most, published unchallenged on a designer's own channel: «дизайнер этого не предусмотрел и вряд ли мог… просто специфика другая». «И вряд ли мог» is the load-bearing part — a scope limit on the design project as a deliverable, not a complaint about one designer.** **⚠️ And her answer to the hardest intake problem — briefing storage for a flat nobody has lived in: analyse the CURRENT home for where mess actually accumulates, and notice where you habitually throw a charger or a t-shirt, then legitimise storage there. Design for observed behaviour, which is evidence the client has already produced, rather than for stated need.** Governing principle: **«порядок зависит от того, удобно ли размещены вещи»** — a function of placement, not household size. **The verdict on the video's own question is the useful conclusion: family-scenario design is not a different method for larger households, it is the same method applied to OVERLAPPING scenarios — «сценарии те же самые, просто их много, и они пересекаются».** Plus: **placement follows the action sequence** (pots nearest the water because you fill first) and **storage height follows who uses it, and changes** (plates moved down when the daughter started serving herself); **a household protocol rather than a shelf** — the reserves shelf with the family rule «если здесь нет, значит нужно заказать»; **«ящик позора», an explicit concession that total order is not the goal**; **⚠️ the drying failure, the round's best unrecoverable-planning case: no dryer, never briefed, now technically impossible to add, so a rack stands permanently in the bedroom — and the designer's generalisation names the sequence («заказывают обычную стиральную машину и про сушилку вообще не думают»), with the rule that laundry equipment must be in the ТЗ**; **the knock-on that it becomes a BATHROOM storage problem**; **a hockey санузел built for one family once the scenario WAS briefed**; **three children's rules with an incident behind them — involve the child from 2–3, never touch or discard another person's things, and the grandmother who tidied a 15-year-old's room while she was out**; **a 9 m² kids room called their own mistake, against the pattern that the older the child the less parents invest**; **⚠️ an argument AGAINST built-in fixed dividers, because the composition of what you own changes**; and **a genuine counter-data-point on ЛДСП: a self-built shoe rack in the cheapest board with edges entirely unsealed, wet winter shoes, no swelling — one uninstrumented household observation, recorded with its informality, against the blanket «ЛДСП всё» claim.** [[_Sources/YT_9ZwqMKiMgvc_nsdsgn_storage_organiser_family_flat|note]].

- `yt_-6QKr4HerCQ` — «Как заказать идеальную кухню? 25 ошибок» (2025-07-22) — **FULL (fact_yield 28), `promotional_ratio: medium`, `corroborates_existing: true`. Solo host. Selected DELIBERATELY to measure duplication against a subject this vault already covers heavily, and the result is the round's most informative mixed answer.** **⚠️ No absolute prices at all — every figure is a ratio or a dimension, which makes the set unusually portable and non-decaying**: installation **~10%** of the kitchen, non-standard **+40% minimum**, built-in microwave **2–3×**, dark ДСП/PVC **2–3× cheaper** than matte enamel or Fenix, European vs Chinese hardware **~10×**, gloss and matte the same price on MDF, a ready cabinet «на порядок дешевле» than bespoke, one large item cheaper than several small. **⚠️ Format is the finding: every one of the 25 items carries the mistake, the fix AND «на чём можно сэкономить» — and the cost-down advice repeatedly steers AWAY from spend, including away from work a designer would bill for.** Clearances with their failure modes: **hob to furniture/fridge 300 mm minimum** (else you permanently soil the fridge side with food and steam), **sink to hob 600 mm absolute minimum / 1,000 mm ideal**, uppers **500–600 mm** above the countertop, **300–500 mm free air above a niche-fitted free-standing fridge** (materially more than this vault's existing 200 mm from Кузина — recorded as a divergence, not reconciled), **standard heights 2,210/2,400 mm with no three-metre standard kitchen**. **⚠️ The timed check: demand the production drawing before facades are cut, read the opening triangles, verify door clashes AND handle-depth clearance, and confirm the доборные глухари are on it.** **⚠️ The hardware rule that replaces "never cheap out": spend by FREQUENCY of use — good hardware on the fridge and dishwasher, cheapest on a drawer opened once a year — evidenced by his own reversal on push-open, one Blum tip-on in constant use for eighteen months against cheap ones that «постоянно ломались».** Plus **the two-tier upper cabinet retired by its own user and dated to ~2020, with the exposed торец named as the worst execution failure**; **appliances selected FIRST because the kitchen is built to them and because they now often cost more than the kitchen**, with his own caveat that price does not guarantee appliance longevity; **the convection mechanism for built-in ventilation gaps (perforation below as an inlet, not only a gap above)**; **the contested under-sink volume — bin vs измельчитель vs osmosis tanks**; and **the honest flag that pull-out drawers are the one recommendation with no saving attached.** *Corroborating rather than new: the work triangle, the 500–600 mm upper clearance, matte over gloss, sockets and worktop LED as never-cut, plan-before-buy.* [[_Sources/YT_-6QKr4HerCQ_nsdsgn_kitchen_25_ordering_mistakes|note]].

- `yt_gTgUJaWKobM` — «Что такое современный натяжной потолок? Теневое примыкание» (2023-01-25) — **FULL (fact_yield 31), `promotional_ratio: medium`, `corroborates_existing: true`. Manual Russian caption track.** Guest Алексей Морозов, head of installer СС Групп, filmed in the profile manufacturer's showroom; **the brand is ASR-garbled throughout ("crap"/"crop"/"krab Systems") and recorded as probably Krab Systems, `uncertain` — the dimensions are stated numerically and unaffected.** **⚠️ `теневое примыкание` returned ZERO vault matches and `shadow gap` only ten, against 150 files mentioning stretch ceilings — the general subject was covered, this detail was not.** **⚠️ The most useful passage is a claim WITH its own on-camera correction: 27 mm minimum drop «в идеальных условиях» is pushed back on by the host (a распределительная коробка is ~60 mm), and the qualification survives — the drop is measured FROM THE LOWEST OBSTRUCTION, not the slab; standard systems drop 40–60 mm from that point, the Slim 27 line 20–27 mm. So it must NOT be compared with this vault's slab-referenced ceiling-drop ranking.** Jan 2023 prices at 62.2457 RUB/USD: mass-market **250–300 (≈$4–$5/m²)**, PVC fabric alone **~1,000 (≈$20)**, **PVC on shadow gap ~2,500 (≈$40)**, textile alone **~2,500 (≈$40)**, **textile on shadow gap ~4,000–4,500 (≈$60–$70)**, plasterboard turnkey **5,000–7,000 (≈$80–$110)** — his plasterboard figure sourced by surveying partner прорабы rather than asserted, with the quality condition built into the question. **⚠️ Fabric is only 10–20% of a ceiling project's cost — a ~1,000,000 RUB (≈$16,100) project carried ~100,000 RUB (≈$1,600) of fabric — which is what makes the repair rule tolerable (damage within 50–100 mm of a wall re-cuts in place; nearer the centre means replacing the membrane).** **⚠️ Natural sag ≈1% of the room diagonal, and it does NOT increase over time — growing sag is a diagnostic for a non-airtight ceiling void, which is the THIRD independent source in this vault for the billowing mechanism and the first to name a product response (an «Aircraft» profile venting the junction).** Plus **a directly usable clearance: 30 mm minimum between a hinged wardrobe door and the membrane, or run a light line along the wardrobe to support the fabric and get down to 5–10 mm**; **width limits PVC 5.5 m, textile 4.90 m usable, with the seam handled as a design decision (shadow divider, light line, level change) rather than a weld**; **free-standing columns needing a two-sided profile and leaving a visible dark seam — he met it for the first time on a 60 m² room**; **a 1.5 × 1.5 m inspection hatch the plasterboard crew said was impossible**; **10–15 working days lead time and «настройка начинается всегда с вентиляции»**; **yellowing CONCEDED from smoke, which means this vault should not carry "does not yellow" unqualified**; **mass-market explicitly endorsed for a rental flat — a supplier steering out of his own segment**; and **the generalised warning that minimalist profile details look trivial and must be agreed before the crew is on site, independently corroborating the builder's «многие впервые его делают» on теневой профиль.** [[_Sources/YT_gTgUJaWKobM_nsdsgn_stretch_ceiling_shadow_gap|note]].

- `yt_pR1t7zlT2Qk` — «Стеклянные перегородки: дорого или нет? 10 мифов» (2026-07-14) — **FULL (fact_yield 19), `promotional_ratio: HIGH — the highest of the round.** Guest Руслан from Декус (a DIFFERENT Руслан from `M0Wv4SOSUhs`), filmed in their showroom, with mid-video plugs for their furniture, a collaboration brand, three showrooms and a long closing pitch for the host's own studio. **⚠️ This was the round's sharpest tier-steering test and it FAILS the price question cleanly: the title asks «дорого или нет?» and the video never gives a single figure — not one price for a partition, a door or a square metre. The "expensive?" myth is answered entirely with a cost-STRUCTURE argument that justifies the price rather than quoting it.** **⚠️ What partly redeems it, and it is behavioural: two of the ten myths are CONCEDED as true rather than debunked — dust on horizontal glazing bars is real and needs wiping roughly monthly («друзья, пыль существует, это не миф»), and painting metal well is genuinely hard, with their own degrease→prime→powder-coat process and the observation that many skip priming because some paint makers say it is unnecessary.** Real content: **acoustics, the first hard numbers in this vault for a glazed divider — 8 mm ≈ an average interior door, two 4 mm panes as триплекс ≈40 dB, triplex with an acoustic interlayer up to 50 dB, mechanism correctly given as increasing mass** — **⚠️ but he glosses 50 dB as «в половину», which is wrong because dB are logarithmic; only the interpretation is flagged, the ratings are ordinary specifications**; **span limits that decide the material — aluminium unstable above 4 m on a thin profile, steel practically unlimited with 6 m and 12 m runs built** (a stated glass-thickness ceiling of «80 мм» is implausible and recorded `ASR-uncertain`); **a logistics constraint designers forget — a single oversize pane needed cranes and a removed window to enter the flat**; **stainless or aluminium for wet rooms, safety glass only**; **the cost structure with named failure points — closers few outside Italy and Germany last 5 years, and cheap soft rollers go oval so the partition starts jumping within about two months**; **«нет стоимости без конкретной позиции», which the host explicitly generalises to renovation costing**; and **the fashion-versus-style distinction («мода скоротечная… металл, дерево, мрамор — это уже стиль»)** with the structural market driver that living space is shrinking while its price rises. One incidental durability finding kept because the mechanism is specific: **they abandoned ordinary furniture leather after real рекламации — wet hair and a silk robe transferred dye — switching to a deep-matte automotive leather.** [[_Sources/YT_pR1t7zlT2Qk_nsdsgn_glass_partitions_10_myths|note]].

### ВАШ ДИЗАЙНЕР ИНТЕРЬЕРА / NSDSGN — Round 2, 5 videos (2026-09-03)

**⚠️ Round 2 was composed entirely from GUEST formats and the trades, on Round 1's own finding that this channel's content is carried by its guests rather than its host.** Round 1's prediction was that yield would fall below 28.7; **it did not — Round 2 came in at 29.0.** The prediction was wrong because Round 1 assumed it had taken the channel's strongest titles, when what it had actually produced was a better *selection rule*, and the channel turns out to be deeper in guest content than assumed. Full round detail in `_Inbox/planning/yourinteriordes_channel_triage_20260903.md`.

- `yt_SEdNCGA0Ttg` — «КАК ВЫБРАТЬ СТРОИТЕЛЕЙ И НЕ ПОЖАЛЕТЬ?» (2024-08-15) — **FULL (fact_yield 33), `promotional_ratio: low`, `corroborates_existing: true`. The round's strongest source and the densest contracting material in this vault.** A 70-minute three-way roundtable; **the key participant is Сергей Аскорбин, who ran renovations as a прораб before opening a design studio**, so the panel contains an insider on the trade being discussed. Region **level 1**. **⚠️ Four GREEN flags, which this vault was short of — it was rich in red flags: he does not agree straight off the bat; he is always booked («любой хороший специалист — у него всегда очередь, 100%»); he ASKS to see the object; and he estimates slowly and ASKS QUESTIONS BACK — «этой информации в проекте нету, как же мне составить смету?» — the returning questions being the diagnostic.** **⚠️ Two proposed signals the panel rejects among themselves and are therefore recorded as CONTESTED, not rules: social-media absence (one calls it a 100% red flag, the other two reject it outright) and the builder's car (flagged as odd by the panel in the same breath).** **⚠️ The cleverest item is an INVERTED test: one designer has never had a brigade refuse to walk him onto a live object, while his own clients frequently refuse to let him show their flat — so a builder who walks anyone onto any site without asking the owner will do the same with yours.** **⚠️ Estimate comparison: send the same PROJECT to every brigade rather than circulating one смета, because otherwise the quotes are not commensurable at all; the same object can legitimately be costed as economy, dearer or luxe BY THE SAME BUILDER; and a good company may deliberately UNDERSTATE to create pleasant expectations.** **⚠️ With a real market friction attached: прорабы increasingly refuse to estimate at all, because the client surveys five quotes and they lose four days — so shopping many quotes can lose you the good contractors. Their fix: make estimating a separate paid service.** **⚠️ ТЕХНАДЗОР gets its own dedicated treatment here and it is unusually balanced — they advocate it and then dismantle it: engage early, never let the builder supervise himself, and their own INTERNAL supervisor failed after about six months because «они с парами уже там друзья»; the market is mostly ex-прорабы selling punishment («выбью с ваших строителей все деньги»); a client-appointed supervisor demanded all the electrics be redone on скрутка instead of Wago clamps, which is thermally WRONG and carried a 500-vs-1,000 RUB per-joint incentive; replacement takes 2–3 months during which the site stands still. Governance fix: журнал, акты, предписание, and a NAMED party taking responsibility in writing.** **⚠️ And the most transferable technical point in the source: СНиП is too loose to be a quality standard — «по СНиПу можно сдать любой объект, самые кривые», at 5 mm per metre, and developers hand over on exactly that. "We comply with СНиП" is not a quality claim; the criteria must be технические карты or the contract.** **⚠️ Warranty: ~3 years normal, ~2 years the stated minimum and «меньше 2 лет — сразу до свидания», split into a general engineering warranty versus material wear from living, which is local restoration not warranty — with matt paint as the modern example that generates disputes that are not warranty issues.** **⚠️ Исполнительная документация: «я за всё своё время работы не сталкивался с реальной ситуацией, когда какой-либо исполнитель давал мне какую-либо исполнительную документацию. Никогда не было такого» — with the five-years-later consequence, and three fixes ranked: photo-fixation with pencil-marked dimensions, a 3D scan kept as a POINT CLOUD and deliberately not modelled («стоит копейки»), or falling back on the installation rules themselves (vertical штробы per ПУЭ, junction boxes within ~150 mm of a stretch-ceiling edge) plus a detector.** Plus: **no vocational construction education exists — he met exactly ONE formally qualified tradesperson in his whole career, which is why he abandoned franchising**; the panel's ranked answer on where to find builders, ending in «trust the designer, then interrogate the recommendation»; **~90% of their clients now ask «у вас есть строители?»**; **a builders' forum coaching people to «просто продавайте за 60»**, i.e. price positioning taught independent of capability; why the designer should NOT contract the builders («нормы прибыли другие»); and **a flipping case study whose lesson is that the FIRST stage was already diagnostic — «по заливке стяжки можно было понять, что ребята не квалифицированы».** [[_Sources/YT_SEdNCGA0Ttg_nsdsgn_choosing_builders_roundtable|note]].

Recorded as mutual corroboration.** **Full all-in design-led ladder, Oct 2023 at 88.1418: basic-but-designed ~120,000 RUB/m² (≈$1,400), middle/business ~180,000–220,000 (≈$2,000–$2,500), 250,000+ (≈$2,800+); labour-only SPb ~25,000–35,000 (≈$280–$400) and Moscow ~35,000–55,000 (≈$400–$620), plus 15,000–20,000 черновые on top, plus +20% for complex work.** **⚠️ Scope warning recorded prominently: Round 1's contractor ladder is construction work ONLY, this one is all-in including materials, furnishing and the designer — the ~10× gap is scope, not disagreement.** **⚠️ The best insight is counter-intuitive and mechanical: a MINIMALIST interior costs MORE, not less — «минималистичные интерьеры тебя не прощают недорогих подходов… когда у тебя пустой интерьер, все косяки мебели, плохие стыковки нескольких материалов в одной точке — это всё заметно». Minimalism removes the visual noise that hides poor execution, so it raises the minimum acceptable trade quality. Real case at 250,000–270,000 RUB/m², 135–140 m², microcement throughout, no doors at all. And the mirror failure: chasing luxe in a detail-heavy style while economising yields «некая пародия настоящего дорогого проекта».** **⚠️ AND THE ITEM WITH THE WIDEST CONSEQUENCE FOR THIS WHOLE VAULT — a procurement-commission disclosure volunteered by designers against their industry: designers hold agency agreements with suppliers, and the markup is named from the builder side as «закинь там ещё мои 10–15», with some at 2% and some at 3%. Their stated alternative is to pass the lowest negotiated price through unmarked and charge the client a percentage of that reduced total, with every month's receipts on a shared drive for verification. → Any material or supplier recommendation sourced from a designer anywhere in this vault may carry an undisclosed 2–15% commission.** Plus **a ~500,000 RUB (≈$5,700) absolute total for a small panel-building bathroom**; **«всё дорожает, кроме дизайн-проекта»**; **match the designer to your BUDGET and STYLE because cost-optimisation is a distinct skill — «Дима просто не сможет, он не ездит по Леруа Мерлен»**; and **independent corroboration of Round 1's never-economise rule, via the cheapest pipes under the most expensive radiator that leaked in six months.** [[_Sources/YT_yaeQr8Q0tCQ_nsdsgn_renovation_cost_roundtable|note]].

- `yt_xA0pFTLCt5M` — «ВЕНТИЛЯЦИЯ и КОНДИЦИОНИРОВАНИЕ В КВАРТИРЕ» (2023-10-17) — **FULL (fact_yield 30), `promotional_ratio: low`, `corroborates_existing: true`. Ducts are installed FIRST, and «очень часто этого пространства не хватает для установки лампочек или треков» because the lighting arrives after the ceilings.** **⚠️ Independent corroboration of the combined ventilation-plus-track slot Round 1's ceiling installer demonstrated in a showroom — «сейчас даже есть решение, когда щель вентиляции стыкуется с шинопроводом» — two trades, two roles, same product, and the designer's version confirms it is specified in practice.** Plus **a free acceptance test: hold a sheet of paper to the ventilation grille, «если прилип — вентиляция отличная»**; **the prohibition on connecting a cooker hood into passive channels, which neighbours ignore, «и вы чувствуете запах их пищи или, ещё хуже, никотина» — a building defect no work inside your own flat can fix**; **КИВ supply air arriving at street temperature and contamination, so «я много раз видел, что клапаны просто замуровывают»**; **noise as a flat diagnostic — «если вы слышите шум вентиляции, это значит только одно: она спроектирована неправильно»**, with cross-talk between rooms as a PRIVACY failure (a neighbour heard the owner talking through the kitchen extract); **a 2nd-floor-in-a-12-storey flat needing a very expensive outdoor unit purely to pump the freon line**; **a legally protected facade as a hard prohibition**; and **two ventilation-contractor failure modes — the crew that vanishes before commissioning, and the one that forgot to connect the AC condensate drain and left, after which everything was tiled over. That second one is the same failure class Round 1's builder described from the opposite side.** [[_Sources/YT_xA0pFTLCt5M_nsdsgn_ventilation_and_ac_costs|note]].

- `yt_cudO-SSdrn0` — «Вы неправильно храните вещи! Полезные приемы от дизайнера» (2026-02-03) — **FULL (fact_yield 28), `promotional_ratio: low`, `corroborates_existing: true`. Third cause: marketplace storage-lifehack content, which she rejects outright — «на самом деле это всё не работает, ребята».** **⚠️ The most transferable principle is a sizing rule: «мелкие категории, но большие контейнеры — и всё равно бардак». Match container size to CATEGORY size.** **⚠️ Product verdicts with reasons, which is what makes the format worth processing — endorsed: a 45 cm wheeled container with repositionable dividers (bought «десятками» for every project, two fit a kitchen drawer), a spring-loaded expanding divider that works in every zone, modular bathroom trays assembled as a mosaic, in-cabinet rotating stands (but not on a countertop), adhesive mop and cloth holders. Rejected with reasons: hanging baskets, multi-level pyramids, and the notched marketplace dividers that «разбалтываются и свою функцию никаким образом не выполняют».** **⚠️ A clearance mechanism new to this vault: she does NOT recommend hinged facades on 600 mm kitchen uppers «потому что они бьют в голову» — and it does not happen in his kitchen because he made the countertop 100 mm DEEPER. Extra countertop depth buys upper-door head clearance.** **⚠️ A genuine PERSPECTIVES case neither concedes: he bought Blum's native hidden internal drawer and is happy; she has no complaint about the tray («почти нет мёртвых зон») but rejects the TYPE — «добраться до второго ящика это большой труд» — and wants «обычные настоящие ящики, каждый со своим фасадом». Façade continuity against movements-per-use, and the same trade-off recurs in the entrance hall where she says «я знаю, что дизайнеры против».** **⚠️ Rail-height arithmetic, diagnosed rather than asserted: his two-tier short-hanging arrangement is correct and «практически для всех ростов доступна», yet the top rail is already out of reach for her at average height — because a 150–180 mm plinth at the BOTTOM spent the height that the top needed. Every millimetre below the lower rail comes off the reachability of the upper one. Plus a separate finding that a shelf ABOVE the rail «обзор перекрывает».** **⚠️ And a scope limit that only emerges from reading BOTH storage videos together: she recommends vertical/file storage generally, and Round 1 recorded her own household ABANDONING it for the husband's t-shirts because they were his highest-frequency item. Scoped rule, not universal.** Plus **a three-year velvet-hanger durability report (nothing slips off; everything gets covered, and cheap ones shed onto shelves), the брючные плечики that let you throw trousers over without unhooking, staggered two-row shoe storage, a shoe-rack width check («измерьте свои кроссовки»), a flat siphon to preserve vanity drawer volume, and her professional tool case — including a BATTERY TESTER, because the hard part of decluttering is deciding.** [[_Sources/YT_cudO-SSdrn0_nsdsgn_storage_organiser_audits_designer|note]].

### ВАШ ДИЗАЙНЕР ИНТЕРЬЕРА / NSDSGN — Round 3 (FINAL), 5 videos (2026-09-03)

**⚠️ Round 3 was scoped at the close of Round 2 as the final round: the four remaining long-form site-review / own-object formats plus ONE probe of the untested mistakes-and-regrets block.** Yields: 28.7 → 29.0 → **29.6.** **Three rounds, 16 videos, 465 facts, no decay in any round — and my decay prediction was wrong all three times.** Full detail and the revised closing recommendation in `_Inbox/planning/yourinteriordes_channel_triage_20260903.md`.

**⚠️ The probe REVERSED the assumption it was sent to test, and that is the round's most consequential outcome — see `2vyIWKmrSXM` below.**

**⚠️ Guest-identity caveat applying to three of the five, per the preflight step adopted after Round 2**: the three site reviews feature a contractor named **Руслан**, **probably but not confirmably the Round 1 builder** (`M0Wv4SOSUhs`). Same first name, same role, same channel, same joint-project relationship — but no старый фонд reference, no 30-plus-years claim, no Петровская коса, and a company name («esus», ASR-garbled) the earlier source did not give. **Treated as probably the same practitioner; overlapping claims recorded as same-practitioner consistency, not independent corroboration.** Little turns on it, as the content is almost entirely new.

> [!NOTE]
> **⚠️ PARTIALLY RESOLVED IN ROUND 4 — recorded here rather than rewritten, so the reasoning stays visible.** Round 4's `AH1INy0i5lU` is filmed **on the Петровская коса object**, with **Руслан as генподрядчик** and the company name rendered «срус» (consistent with the earlier «esus» garble). Round 1's Руслан had said «ещё один наш общий с Русланом объект по нашему дизайн-проекту сейчас реализуется на **Петровской косе**». **→ The Round 1 practitioner and the Round 4 practitioner are CONFIRMED the same person, by object.**
>
> **What this does NOT settle:** Round 3's `2B67955_Z5I` is ЖК «Фамилия», a different object. Given that Round 1's Руслан is a генподрядчик who does joint projects with this designer and is now confirmed on Петровская коса, ЖК «Фамилия» is **very likely** him too — **upgraded from "probably" to "very likely", still not independently proven.** The Round 3 treatment (same-practitioner consistency, not independent corroboration) stands unchanged for the two site reviews.

- `yt_2vyIWKmrSXM` — «20 ошибок, о которых жалеют после ремонта и как их исправить» (2022-10-25) — **FULL (fact_yield 32), `promotional_ratio: low`, `corroborates_existing: true`. Manual ru track.** **⚠️ SENT AS A SINGLE PROBE TO DECIDE WHETHER TO DISMISS EIGHT NEAR-IDENTICAL TITLES, AND IT REVERSED THE VERDICT. It is not a listicle: it is a FOLLOW-UP built out of the comment section of the first such video — «выпуск вызвал огромный резонанс, зрители стали писать о своей боли, делиться опытом своего ремонта, и я решил записать новый выпуск, где подробно разберу эти ситуации». So the content is CROWD-SOURCED POST-OCCUPANCY REGRET DATA with a practitioner's remedy attached to each item — a content type this vault has almost none of, because nearly every source in it describes DECISIONS rather than OUTCOMES.** Highlights: **⚠️ a free-standing bath in a small bathroom, with a reader's unfixable complaint (dirt accumulates behind it and she physically cannot reach it) and the named remedy — a HALF-free-standing bath adjoining the wall on one side, siliconed, which «внешне смотрится практически так же»**; **⚠️ dark GLOSSY floor tile identified as the SINGLE most-written-about regret in his comments, which makes it frequency data rather than opinion**; **⚠️ no underfloor heating under tile, with the balcony mechanism «плита ещё будет примерзать с торца» and the asymmetry that by the time you feel it «будет уже поздно что-то менять»**; **⚠️ the owner's HEIGHT ignored, in both directions (a 2 m client: «вы не представляете, сколько в Петербурге мест, где можно удариться головой»), with Neufert's dimensions criticised as insufficient — «интерьер должен соответствовать своему хозяину, как обувь или одежда» — and a worked case where a 3.2 m ceiling got a 2 m kitchen because the short client would never climb to the antresols**; **⚠️ a comfort-beats-looks rule resting on REPAIRABILITY (a comfortable sofa can be re-upholstered; wrong geometry cannot be fixed) plus a testing protocol for when the actual item cannot be sat on**; **⚠️ THE LOGISTICS SET, with exact numbers — a large washing machine is a 60×60 cm cube, a bathroom door LEAF is also often 60 cm, and «полотно 60 закрывается ещё ушами наличников» so it will NOT pass; large Italian sofas and beds come in THROUGH THE WINDOW («представьте, если у вас 15–20 этаж»); and the practice worth copying, from their stone contractors, who «всегда приезжают заранее, проверяют маршрут, обязательно замеряют ЛИФТ, смотрят черновую лестницу»**; **⚠️ a flat refusal of сололифт on a jurisdictional ground — «по закону управляющая компания может на одни сутки легко отключать электричество», and for that day the pump does not work — plus «вспомните, когда вы пошли в туалет в самолёте»**; **⚠️ leak sensors endorsed WITH a product warning that is the valuable half: AliExpress Bluetooth WIRELESS sensors that began shutting the water off whenever bathroom humidity rose, «единственный способ отключить было запихнуть его в коробку с рисом» — the SECOND independent false-triggering account on this channel**; **⚠️ a designer arguing AGAINST the master switch (you will never switch off the fridge; LED lighting draws too little to matter) and redirecting to the function that matters — «совсем другое дело, уезжая, перекрыть горячую и холодную воду» — with a breaker-labelling retrofit for a finished flat**; **⚠️ THE ŠTROBA MAP, the zero-cost tier of a problem this channel raises three times: while the chases are open, pencil the dimensions on the wall («от этого угла 50 см») and PHOTOGRAPH it — «и у вас будет карта всех штроб вашей будущей квартиры» — extended to закладные and STUDS, with a real case of his tiler asking for exactly that photo**; **⚠️ night light versus navigation light defined and separated**; **⚠️ LED brightness declared unpredictable on paper, making a dimmer a CALIBRATION device — with cases of clients who refused one and needed an electrician back afterwards**; **⚠️ DECIDE WHO DECIDES before the work starts, with the compounding failure (six months of design, then the wife joins, then the children) producing «какое-то непонятное болото, не похожее вообще ни на что», the designer refusing to be recruited as an ally («ты должен поддержать мою идею, я тебе подмигну»), and the remedy he endorses — SPLIT THE DOMAINS, appearance to one spouse and function/engineering to the other**; **⚠️ DESIGNING DURING PREGNANCY as a repeatedly-observed intake risk — consistently «кричащий, на грани» projects that clients regretted a year later, one client saying so about herself in advance — with the remedy of holding to a classical style**; мы все живые люди — кто-то может заболеть, кто-то может переехать»**; and **⚠️ the УПРАВЛЯЮЩАЯ КОМПАНИЯ relationship as a schedule-risk asset nothing else in this vault names — the УК sees «человеческий подход» and grants preferences that are all schedule items: the building's own plumber or electrician when something must be shut off, waste removal, and OUT-OF-HOURS deliveries.** Rough materials noted as having risen «чуть не в два раза» over the preceding year.
