## Round 4 — ВАШ ДИЗАЙНЕР ИНТЕРЬЕРА / NSDSGN (Александр Синчуков), 5 sources, 2026-09-03

**Scoped on the CORRECTED yield model from Round 3 — select by FORMAT, not by what is left.** Three picks from the mistakes-and-regrets block that Round 3's probe reclassified from dismiss to process (taken longest-first, including the series' ORIGINAL that the already-processed follow-up was a response to); one guest format never triaged into any round; one deliberate dedup test on the vault's densest subject. **Round 4 yield: 5 videos, 180 facts, 36.0/video — the highest of the four rounds, and the fourth consecutive round in which my predicted decay did not occur.**

- `yt_RxU4L7ce86E` — «Детская комната глазами психолога» (2023-11-21, 69 min) — **FULL (fact_yield 42 — the highest of any source from this channel), `promotional_ratio: low`, `corroborates_existing: partial`. Manual ru track. Guest: Наталья Степанова, clinical psychologist, 20 years in child and adult psychotherapy, director of a psychological centre. Guest-name preflight run per the Round 2 lesson: CLEAN, genuinely new voice.** **⚠️ IT IS THE PRIMARY SOURCE FOR A CLAIM THE VAULT HELD SECOND-HAND, AND THE PRIMARY VERSION IS MATERIALLY BETTER: Round 1's storage-organiser source cited «подкаст с детским психологом» for "a child's desk should ideally be white", keeping the colour and losing the reason. The real requirement is that the surface be PLAIN and light so it acts as visual BACKGROUND and the brain concentrates on what is ON it; wood with visible knots is a named failure mode; it matters most for hyperactive and attention-disordered children. The colour was never the point — figure-ground separation is, which also rules out patterned desk mats.** Highlights: **⚠️ a four-interval replacement schedule with 5→7 named as MANDATORY to change («другая система игр») and furniture cadences of every six months to age 3 and two years for a schoolchild — in productive tension with this vault's "design for ~10 years" rule, resolvable by splitting infrastructure from contents**; **⚠️ THE 40 cm DESK, which closes a dimension gap this vault had explicitly flagged — a beautiful 40×100 cm natural-wood desk bought for a 6-year-old starting school, with the enumeration test (тетрадь, рабочая тетрадь, учебник, планшет, ручки, пенал, plus a phone) and the specific geometric failure being the TILTED exercise book: «даже вот так сделать просто не получится ФИЗИЧЕСКИ»**; **⚠️ growing CHAIRS preferred over growing DESKS because of the footrest**; **⚠️⚠️ TWO SEPARATE DESK TOPS, NEVER ONE SHARED WORKTOP, with a client case involving an adult — a mother and teenage daughter on one long top, the daughter colonising it, the mother saying «я-то на неё раздражаюсь» and the psychologist replying «я тоже раздражалась бы». The mechanism generalises far beyond children: a shared surface cannot carry a boundary, so the conflict becomes interpersonal instead of spatial. Adjacency is fine; shared surface is not. She volunteers the adult parallel unprompted — clients asking for «ровно два одинаковых шкафа»**; **⚠️ BUNK BEDS argued against with a blunt physical reason found nowhere else in this vault (the teenager on the lower bunk watching an adult's torso, legs and genitals while the younger child is settled above) and an AREA BUDGET for the alternative — ~2 m² of genuinely personal space each, ~4 m² total plus a passage, «реально даже для совсем маленькой комнаты»**; **⚠️ the storage strategy INVERTING THREE TIMES — closed 0–3 for CURIOSITY («много закрытых дверец, чтобы было ЧТО ОТКРЫВАТЬ»), open 5–7 for independent play, closed 7+ for CONCENTRATION, hands-off in adolescence — which resolves two flatly contradictory rules the vault already held from unrelated channels, and supplies the mechanism behind the one that only stated a conclusion**; **⚠️ toy QUANTITY converting possessions into rubbish in the child's own perception («это СВАЛКА машинок, в которых он не ориентируется… он относиться к этим вещам будет как к БАРАХЛУ»), with the host's own Lego argument as the generalisation — «когда Lego меряется на КИЛОГРАММЫ, на ВЕС, это перестаёт быть ценностью и становится РАСХОДНЫМ МАТЕРИАЛОМ»**; **⚠️ the ROBOT MURAL conflict adjudicated against another psychologist's advice, distinguishing an inanimate figure painted BY AN ADULT (not a risk factor) from a child spontaneously DRAWING living creatures fused with inanimate parts (a referral signal) — and then raising a BETTER objection, that any character scheme for a 6-year-old is «примерно на ГОД» because school entry changes the thinking, with the Красная шапочка ticket test**; **⚠️ the neutral-wall display CORD as the design answer, with autobiographical objects rather than bought decoration**; **⚠️ bright nurseries objected to on TWO grounds, the second being that ready-made imagery STOPS imagination**; **⚠️ a dark-blue 7-year-old's room where homework was impossible («я достаточно трудоспособный человек, но я в этой комнате тоже заниматься не могла») — independent corroboration of this vault's existing "gloomy forest" critique, now with a functional consequence**; **⚠️ gender colour coding refuted flatly («в генетическом коде это не прописано») and wall colour declared NOT physiologically active while LIGHT is (brightness and flicker), which is a useful boundary on the whole colour-psychology question**; **⚠️ a lock on a teenager's door reframed as a SYMPTOM of entry without knocking, with knocking recommended from age 7–8 as a two-way courtesy — which upgrades a claim this vault held as one designer's «мне кажется» to a corroborated specification item**; **⚠️ a teenager's mess named as a SEPARATION INSTRUMENT, making enforcement self-defeating («не будет застилать НАЗЛО ВАМ, даже если его самого будет раздражать») with the design consequence that teenage storage must work without parental enforcement**; **⚠️⚠️ SHE REFUSES TO NAME A THRESHOLD AGE for separating opposite-sex children, and says why — «я скажу, что важно до 7 лет отселить, и у них НЕВРОЗ появится из-за того, что они не могут» — declining to publish a norm that would only generate guilt in families who cannot act on it, and reframing the requirement as ACCESS TO PRIVACY rather than a separate room**; **⚠️⚠️ AND SHE REFUSES THE DESIGN FRAMING of "the child never uses the room we built him", dismantling the premise arithmetically down to roughly ONE EVENING HOUR of shared family time — a direct disagreement with the interviewing designer's own published answer to the same complaint, recorded as a Perspectives split**; **⚠️⚠️ «как не развестись после ремонта», which the host says was the ONE question all subscribers asked instead of anything about children — two of her client couples divorced during renovations, with perfectionism identified as a RELATIONAL rather than a quality problem, and the causal loop that the withdrawal the managing partner resents is PRODUCED by the same perfectionism that makes them manage it alone**; **⚠️⚠️ and the round's best synthesis — a brief built from BORROWED IMAGES rather than lived experience, which is the single root cause of three separately-recorded failures on this vault (the unused gym and cinema, the magazine self-levelling floor, the Pinterest bathroom), with the HOTEL-ROOM TEST as a concrete briefing antidote and the conclusion that radical design is a PORTFOLIO decision — acceptable across several properties, not with one.** [[_Sources/YT_RxU4L7ce86E_nsdsgn_child_psychologist_kids_rooms|note]].

- `yt_CSpXvPWpsgQ` — «15 вещей, о которых жалеют после ремонта» (2022-09-20, 27 min) — **FULL (fact_yield 34), `promotional_ratio: low`, `corroborates_existing: partial`. Manual ru track.** **⚠️ THE SERIES ORIGINAL. The already-processed `2vyIWKmrSXM` (2022-10-25) is its follow-up, built from this video's comment section and billed as «ещё 20» — so the items are DISTINCT BY CONSTRUCTION. Selected precisely because the follow-up existed, i.e. the dedup risk was structural rather than probable, and it did not materialise. Evidence class is the highest in this vault: clients living in his own interiors for 3, 5 and 10 years, asked what they regret.** Highlights: **⚠️ the objects that never reach the brief, named as a list — сушилка, робот-пылесос, туалет для кота, поилочка, гладильная доска, кофе-машина, чайник — with a laundry basket that ended up «на самом видном месте» and the right remedy shape: «если вещь не спрятать — давайте делать НА НЕЙ АКЦЕНТ», i.e. three outcomes not two**; **⚠️ CORNER DOORS on a shower enclosure named the worst of all configurations, and the mechanism is continuous by design rather than a seal failure — water sheds off both leaves every time, «у вас постоянно ЛУЖА»**; **⚠️⚠️ A BATH-RIM PULL-OUT SHOWER that ended in demolition — the hose cracked slightly, seeped below any detection threshold, and the sealed box concealed it until «целая жизнь зародилась из чёрной плесени»; not repairable, the bath had to be broken out. Two compounding factors that both generalise: sub-threshold seepage, and an enclosure with no inspection route**; **⚠️ a podium built too low so water stands in the tray, with supervision named explicitly as the remedy («найдите того, кто будет СЛЕДИТЬ за вашим сантехником и проверит все уклоны») and the demolition cost attached — plus the design decision that causes it, «не гонитесь за низкой ванной, пренебрегая инженерией»**; **⚠️ THE FULL DOOR-SWING CLASH CLUSTER — a socket behind a habitually open door; his own switch on the door-handle axis, struck open by the leaf (he twice thought he was being burgled); a cabinet whose internal DRAWER fouls the leaf, a RADIATOR or a WINDOWSILL, correctly attributed to the мебельщик working from real dimensions; a kitchen or fridge meeting a continuing wall, discovered only once delivered and assembled; and handle-and-switch on OPPOSITE sides, with the likely cause being a swing changed mid-project and the socket not moved — which makes it a CHANGE-CONTROL rule, not a layout rule**; **⚠️⚠️ THE WHITE GLOSS POURED FLOOR with THREE independent confirmations — dark streaks from any contact, outright YELLOWING around the floor-recessed convectors, the client's own substitution (large-format white gloss porcelain, «картинка та же при совершенно других свойствах»), the magazine author he later met IN ITALY telling him it looked good «только первую НЕДЕЛЮ, когда его фотографировали», and a restaurant builder who WITHDREW the service because he tired of working at a loss. The one-week figure is the hardest evidence in this vault that published photography is worthless as a durability basis**; **⚠️ permanent designer constructions and the reversibility argument (a bed podium level with the windowsill; a princess's castle serving a year or two, whose removal destroys the whole nursery)**; **⚠️ TWO PAINT CASES — marking white walls where the client explicitly ruled out the colour and blamed the cheap paint, resolved by covering every wall in decorative plaster «и проблемы просто исчезли»; and a matte BLACK wall leaving an unwashable whitish soapy patch. The physics for the second arrives from the same practitioner four years later, so the vault now holds case and mechanism**; **⚠️ natural-material properties clients refuse to believe (brass blackens, copper greens, wood dents under thin heels, kitchen marble «превратился во что-то ужасное») with the framing that an expensive thing needs MORE delicate treatment, not less, and the honest alternative stated rather than hidden**; **⚠️ "one room short" as a career-long invariant («даже если у них десятикомнатная квартира, им не будет хватать одиннадцатой») and its mirror image, the gym or cinema nobody uses**; **⚠️ TEMPORARY FURNITURE, with a discriminator better than the prohibition — a GOOD mattress on the floor that later went into the eventual bed is a COMPONENT of the final solution; a cheap sofa is a SUBSTITUTE for it, and that is the one that never leaves**; **⚠️ TOO MANY SWITCHES (real use collapses to two or three scenarios; a minute spent hunting in a large house) set against TOO FEW SOCKETS, with the nursery as the strongest case and the paired warning being the more valuable half — fashionable USB sockets and built-ins with a fashion cycle, proved by the ANALOGUE smart home that needed a whole room for a server and is now «просто какой-то атавизм». Rule: the SOCKET is the durable interface, the DEVICE is not**; **⚠️ technical light duplicated by designer light, with the functional split stated cleanly and his own preference for designer light as an ART OBJECT that «еле-еле светит»**; **⚠️⚠️ THREE CONTRACTOR TYPES with the trade-offs attached, AND THE COST MODEL — he discloses he is running the separate-trades option on his own flat and reports that «те деньги, которые сэкономил… они КОМПЕНСИРОВАЛИ все затраты на переделки, потому что все переделки лягут именно на ВАС». The real difference between a main contractor and separate trades is therefore NOT COST but who bears REWORK RISK and coordination load. This is the ex ante half of the retrospective this vault already held from his 55 m² flat, and it closes the argument**; **⚠️⚠️ and BUILT NOT TO THE DESIGN PROJECT — a friend's lamp switching from one point only, blamed on his design until he produced the electrical sheet, with the conclusion that a design project is an EVIDENTIARY ARTEFACT for fault attribution, plus the striking datum that BUILDERS have twice commissioned and PAID FOR a design project themselves because a fixed documented scope converts client-driven changes into chargeable доп работы.** [[_Sources/YT_CSpXvPWpsgQ_nsdsgn_fifteen_post_occupancy_regrets|note]].

- `yt_WCoqOCofPx4` — «Почему ремонт быстро умирает — 10 правил неубиваемого интерьера» (2026-04-14, 33 min) — **FULL (fact_yield 36), `promotional_ratio: low`, `corroborates_existing: partial`. ASR ru track, well punctuated. ⚠️ The ASR renders his surname «Синчуков» — which turned out to be CORRECT; the form this vault used until 2026-09-03 (Сенчугов) was my own Round 1 error, propagated 61 times and corrected vault-wide. See Change_Log.** **⚠️ THE MOST SYSTEMATICALLY STRUCTURED SOURCE ON THIS CHANNEL, and its central thesis is original to this vault: «между уборкой, практичностью и долговечностью есть ПРЯМАЯ СВЯЗЬ. Чем ЧАЩЕ вы убираетесь, тем БЫСТРЕЕ вы РАЗРУШАЕТЕ интерьер… чем интерьер РАСПОЛОЖЕН к тому, чтобы убираться меньше, тем дольше он проживёт.» → CLEANING IS ITSELF A WEAR MECHANISM, so the target is a surface that NEEDS less cleaning rather than one that tolerates more. That single reframing accounts, as one phenomenon, for five findings the vault previously held as unrelated cautions: matte paint burnishing where wiped, textured panels trapping dust, marble absorbing dirt, facades damaged during CLEANING rather than use, and mechanical ventilation reducing dust. He extends it into a causal chain nothing else in this vault makes — bad LAYOUT → clutter → more cleaning → faster physical destruction — which retroactively justifies the vault's storage-organiser content as a DURABILITY measure. And the closing definition reframes the whole subject: «неубиваемый интерьер — это НЕ когда ничего не ломается; это когда всё можно легко ОБСЛУЖИТЬ, ЗАМЕНИТЬ и ПОЧИНИТЬ.»** Highlights: **⚠️ ZONING BY LOAD as the resolution of the natural-versus-artificial tension — porcelain floor AND walls in the прихожая, and «в зоне СПАЛЬНИ можно РАССЛАБИТЬСЯ, ВЫДОХНУТЬ и использовать краску, паркет и другие ХРУПКИЕ материалы, например мрамор на подоконниках»**; **⚠️ a porcelain-versus-ceramic freeze-thaw distinction with a figure (~100+ cycles, and ordinary ceramic outdoors «несколько раз замёрзнув и разморозившись, просто ТРЕСНЕТ»)**; **⚠️ JOINT COUNT as the real porcelain variable, with large format as the lever and the honest cost balance**; **⚠️ FLOATING FLOORS rejected on a 20-year observation that порожки «ВСЕГДА ОТЛЕТАЮТ — даже те, которые специально ПРИКРУЧИВАЮТСЯ САМОРЕЗАМИ», which is the argument against the usual remedy**; **⚠️ the CONTACT ZONES where finishes actually die — beside handles («особенно у девушек с длинными ногтями… это просто ФАКТ, который мы 100 раз видели») and beside switches, established by a question rather than a claim: «как часто вы попадаете МИМО выключателя? Все говорят: ни разу. Но если присмотреться, ВСЕГДА рядом с выключателем появляются пятна» — with the named detail to refuse being a SWITCH INTEGRATED INTO A MIRROR**; **⚠️ external corners, where one accidental chip forces redecorating BOTH walls**; **⚠️ decorative plaster prescribed as a WEAR LAYER rather than a decorative one, corroborated by the 2022 client outcome**; **⚠️ THE MATTE-PAINT MECHANISM explained correctly as burnishing («шероховатость уходит, и поверхность становится глянцевой»), with ultra-matte declared a CEILING product on a wash-frequency test**; **⚠️ texture cutting both ways — it HIDES marks and TRAPS dust, so use it where a surface is touched and avoid it where it is merely dusted, a reconciliation the source itself does not state**; **⚠️ THE KITCHEN IS THE AGGRESSIVE ENVIRONMENT, NOT THE BATHROOM («там нету раскалённых сковородок, там нет кастрюль, с которых летят пятна»), which inverts most people's intuition and reorders the vault's backsplash ladder — with mosaic and natural stone warned against on grease-ABSORPTION grounds**; **⚠️ MARTINDALE abrasion bands (10,000 / 20,000 / 100,000) — he hedges the name and the ASR garbles it as «мерчендей», recorded with the correction and the flag — and the rule that CURTAIN FABRIC HAS NO SUCH RATING, so using it on seating or a bed «ни в коем случае нельзя»**; **⚠️ ДСП named a SINGLE-USE frame material with a ~6-month failure horizon, and фанера/МДФ/металл as the reliable ones, offered as a question to ask at the point of sale**; **⚠️ HINGE QUANTITY SUBSTITUTING FOR HINGE QUALITY — «основная проблема… это ИХ КОЛИЧЕСТВО; если поставите просто БОЛЬШЕ петель, даже простых, вес равномерно распределится»**; **⚠️⚠️ CLADDING A DOOR LEAF WARPS IT, and the mechanism is ASYMMETRIC LOAD not weight — a mirror or porcelain on one face racks the leaf, «начинающие дизайнеры прикрепили и ЗАБЫЛИ»; the fix is BOTH balancing the other face AND adding hinges**; **⚠️ leaf EDGES as the most vulnerable part, with concealed doors in a METAL carcass named as the durable answer and enamelled doors the worst case — a durability argument for concealed doors this vault's cost-focused treatment lacked**; **⚠️ door STOPS ordered with the doors (the wall AND the leaf are both at risk) plus the site-stage substitute of WRAPPING THE HANDLE in something soft, because stops go on last when the walls are already finished**; **⚠️ FOUR leak-system saves including the one genuinely new failure mode — a COARSE STRAINER that leaked under a mains-side pressure SPIKE, an event outside the flat's control, which defeats the "my installation is careful" argument**; **⚠️ over-pressure named as a WARRANTY-VOIDING condition, and pressure gauges noted as frequently absent**; **⚠️ WATER FILTRATION as sanitaryware protection with a six-month visible proof (pull the strainer in a NEW BUILD and see how rusty it is)**; **⚠️ THREE STANDING-WATER DETAILS and a principle — tile run slightly high so water drains INTO the bath, a shelf not taken to the wall so a thin slot drains it, shelves FALLED — with «нельзя делать ПЛОСКИЕ поверхности, в которых будет застаиваться вода»**; **⚠️ dark glossy sanitaryware refused bluntly («если вы берёте ЧЁРНЫЙ УНИТАЗ, через очень короткое время вы всё проклянёте»)**; **⚠️ SWITCH MECHANISM CYCLE LIFE, 1,000 versus 20,000 operations, with the criterion being the MECHANISM not the faceplate — and a FIRE with a diagnostic signature: a worn mechanism shorted and ignited, announcing itself by SWITCHING THE LIGHT ON while he slept, black smoke five seconds later. A light that comes on by itself is a reason to kill that circuit immediately**; **⚠️ LED drivers required to be VENTILATED, gathered in a separate щит, and LABELLED — the last learned by having to work through every track to add one corridor dimmer**; **⚠️ transparent glass shades covered in dust within a couple of months, with a measurable output loss («света тоже стало гораздо больше») after he removed them all**; **⚠️ MVHR reframed as a DURABILITY investment via the dust-and-cleaning chain («на полу нигде нет пыли… там нужна МИНИМАЛЬНАЯ уборка»)**; **⚠️ a ducted-ventilation SEQUENCING collision named specifically — walls up, then the client asks for it, and «вентканал НЕ ПРОХОДИТ через стену, дизайн потолков РАССЫПАЕТСЯ» — with the rule that it belongs in the ТЕХНИЧЕСКОЕ ЗАДАНИЕ, not the drawings**; **⚠️ concealed cisterns debunked first-hand («кнопка снимается, всё можно починить, ничего не разрушая»), dated to a 1990s-era fear**; **⚠️⚠️ THE HEADLINE RE-RANKING, made after ten rules mostly about wear from pets, children and cleaning: «самые РАЗРУШИТЕЛЬНЫЕ последствия для интерьера происходят НЕ из-за животных, НЕ из-за детей, а из-за АВАРИЙ» — which makes the leak system the FIRST durability purchase, ahead of any material upgrade**; **⚠️⚠️ AND БЛАГОРОДНОЕ СТАРЕНИЕ, where he reverses his own framework and supplies the actual technical principle: «натуральные материалы с естественной НЕОДНОРОДНОСТЬЮ гораздо лучше СКРЫВАЮТ ИЗНОС» — variegation camouflages damage, uniform surfaces advertise it, which explains both why the poured floor failed so visibly and why his own crazed hall marble does not. With «чем ДОРОЖЕ материал, тем дороже его ОБСЛУЖИВАНИЕ — это отличительная черта настоящего премиума» and the model «РЕСТАВРАЦИЯ вместо ЗАМЕНЫ». ⚠️ An internal tension recorded rather than smoothed: earlier in the same source the прихожая is the heaviest-load room and marble is «моментально убито», yet his own hall floor is natural marble deliberately allowed to craze. Both are defensible, and the reconciliation is that "killed" and "acquires patina" are the SAME PHYSICAL PROCESS under two different aesthetic contracts — so the decision is whether the owner has signed the second one.** [[_Sources/YT_WCoqOCofPx4_nsdsgn_durable_interior_ten_rules|note]].

- `yt_lhikl-7c43c` — «Красивые решения, которые оказались ошибкой — год в моей квартире» (2024-07-04, 34 min) — **FULL (fact_yield 35), `promotional_ratio: low`, `corroborates_existing: partial`. Manual ru track. SPb stated.** **⚠️ THE BEST EVIDENCE CLASS AVAILABLE: every item is a DIAGNOSED fault in his own flat with an IMPLEMENTED fix, one year after moving in, and several carry a measured dimension. «Я исправил [всё] — по-моему, две галочки только осталось.» Almost certainly the SAME FLAT as the already-processed `7zpsZtcTkCM` (55 m², Round 3) one year on — it does not repeat the build, it corrects it, so the vault now holds one practitioner's stated intentions AND his one-year outcomes for a single object.** Framing worth keeping: **«не бывает дизайна [и] ремонта без ошибок; те, кто говорят, что бывает — они вам просто ВРУТ.»** Highlights: **⚠️⚠️ HANDLE-LESS GOLA UPPERS RETROFITTED WITH KNOBS, with two failure modes a showroom trial cannot reveal — the dirt comes from CLOSING with greasy fingers, not opening, and with no handle it is INTUITIVELY UNCLEAR WHICH SIDE TO OPEN FROM. «Минимализм минимализмом, но ВЕРХНИЕ РУЧКИ всё-таки я конечно рекомендую чисто из практики.» Independently corroborated as a general rule by his own 2026 source, which adds the nail-breaking mode; and the unifying mechanism across both, plus his push-to-open verdict, is that ANY surface you must TOUCH to open collects dirt — a handle's function is to move that contact off the visible facade**; **⚠️ a PULL-OUT SPRAY mixer rated the single best change, and the reason is cleaning the SINK rather than filling pots — «надо было конечно сразу его брать»**; **⚠️ an aesthetic compromise admitted outright — the hob sits too close to the sink because «мне хотелось СОБЛЮСТИ КРАСОТУ, чтобы все секции были идеальные, вот эти ПО 60 СМ» — the cleanest case in this vault of a module-regularity rule overriding an ergonomic one, by a professional, in his own home; mitigated by two cheap PASSIVE objects (a board that seats into the sink, and a stainless roll-up mat he uses far more often)**; **⚠️⚠️ THE PLATE-DRAINER CABINET DECLARED OBSOLETE and re-planned WITH TIER HEIGHTS offered for reuse — ~18–20 cm / ~25–28 cm / ~14–15 cm — after raising the drainer shelf 15 cm on Moscow clients' advice, which created a cups-and-glasses niche at the easiest reach and a third shelf for rarely-used items. With the principle behind it: «держать ПЕРЕД СОБОЙ то, что постоянно используешь, и НАВЕРХУ то, что используешь редко»**; **⚠️ a designated MESS NICHE behind one door, and hooks on ADHESIVE TAPE for the gloves that can be hidden — with frequency of use deciding which objects can be concealed at all**; **⚠️⚠️ THE SMART-BUTTON RETROFIT PATTERN, which is the most generalisable item: a vintage floor lamp's foot switch was CLAMPED PERMANENTLY ON and a ~1,500–2,000 ₽ smart button put upstream, scheduled to light the lamp half an hour before he gets home. No rewiring, works with vintage or second-hand fittings that will never have smart internals, and the failure mode is simply "the lamp stays on"**; **⚠️ a 20-year-old Avito armchair rebutting comment-section criticism with usage evidence («одно дело ваши ДОМЫСЛЫ, а другое дело ПРАКТИКА — каждый гость постоянно в нём ЗАЛИПАЕТ»), and the planning finding that the ЛЕЖАК the plan called for was never needed to create the место притяжения**; **⚠️ an entrance console at 1 m 10 cm with a raised lip, fixed on DOUBLE-SIDED TAPE deliberately so it can be peeled off and cleaned, plus the unfixed problem that the plastered wall behind it keeps getting dirty and wanted a PANEL**; **⚠️⚠️ THE MOUSTACHE BENCH as the clearest "beautiful but wrong" verdict in this vault — dust adheres and needs constant wiping, the cat clawed it, and what he actually lacks is TWO SHOE ROWS underneath: «банкетка КРУТАЯ, она ЗАДАЁТ СТИЛЬ… но мне кажется, она ДОЛЖНА БЫЛА БЫТЬ БОЛЕЕ ФУНКЦИОНАЛЬНОЙ. СЕЙЧАС ОНА ЧИСТО ПРО ЭСТЕТИКУ.» It adds a third position to this vault's open-versus-closed shoe-storage disagreement that neither camp argues: he chose NEITHER, and concludes the error was having no everyday tier at all**; **⚠️ the ACCESS-FREQUENCY rule for a concealed panel — his picture over the consumer unit and LED drivers hangs on two nails because «туда я вообще никогда не забираюсь», but a METER would need a hinged element**; **⚠️ OPEN SHELVES replaced by drawers, with the fix if you insist on shelves being to HALVE THE PITCH; transparent containers rejected on VISUAL-NOISE grounds given how chromatically chaotic clothing is; the containers LABELLED; and the finding he could not fix — retro-fitting boxes into a carcass «проектировал НЕ ПОД ЯЩИКИ, а ПОД ГАРДЕРОБ» forced a mismatched set, so the decision that had to be early was the storage TYPE, not the volume**; **⚠️ a пуф for "somewhere for tonight's clothes", a real brief item that appears in no standard bedroom brief, with the alternatives ranked by clearance (пуф / valet stand / foot-of-bed bench, the last impossible in a narrow passage)**; **⚠️⚠️ THE 1% DIMMING FLOOR — Xiaomi night-light units chosen because they reach 1%, «и вот это ЕДИНСТВЕННЫЙ вариант, который мне подходит по яркости», after weakest lamps, shades and tinting all «давали НЕ ТО». The bottom of the driver's range, not lamp selection, is what delivers usable night light**; **⚠️⚠️ TWO NIGHT-LIGHT DUTIES WITH OPPOSITE SCHEDULES — bedroom 19:00–23:00 because «ночью он меня РАЗДРАЖАЕТ, я хочу ПОЛНУЮ ТЕМНОТУ», corridor-by-the-bathroom ALL NIGHT for wayfinding. One fitting type, one recess detail, two contradictory requirements**; **⚠️⚠️ A DIMMER OR DRIVER WITH MEMORY, without which every switch-on returns to full brightness and the calibrated scene stops being used**; **⚠️ the bathroom overhead as a CLEANING light only — «это, наверное, ЕДИНСТВЕННЫЙ СЦЕНАРИЙ, в котором он мне нужен» — now the third room in which this pattern appears**; **⚠️ motorised curtains with PERCENTAGE closing singled out as the favourite function, a schedule that opens them by 11:00 «чтобы я не проспал», and the composed effect he rates highest being a two-device interaction (curtains close, a fitting comes on immediately)**; **⚠️⚠️ THE «АЛИСА, СПОКОЙНОЙ НОЧИ» SCENE, which resolves a direct contradiction in this channel's own advice: he argued in 2022 for capping light groups at two or three because hunting for a switch is intolerable, and here reports exactly that problem with many devices — «нужно КАК ДУРАК ХОДИТЬ ПО ВСЕЙ КВАРТИРЕ» — solved by one command. The reconciliation is a single rule: the tolerable number of light groups is set by whether a SCENE can collapse them into one action**; **⚠️ a flat rejection of MOTION SENSORS («я НЕНАВИЖУ… вот это всё МАХАНИЕ РУКАМИ»), recorded as a dissent because they are usually recommended for exactly the corridors and bathrooms he refuses them in**; **⚠️ four appliance schedules whose shared criterion is ACOUSTIC — moving each device's noise into hours when nobody is home — with a 10% winter humidity reading behind the humidifier and an honest scope statement for the robot vacuum («он НЕ ЗАМЕНЯЕТ обычную влажную уборку»)**; **⚠️ ТЮЛЬ added on a second track row after a roller blind failed the daytime-privacy duty, because a blind is BINARY and must be OPERATED — «надо было СРАЗУ это сделать», discovered through a guest room where the eventual occupant was not the person who signed off the window treatment**; **⚠️ NO underfloor heating in his bathroom, left as an OPEN question he refuses to settle («до сих пор не решил, правильно я сделал или неправильно») after five years without it in a previous flat — with the mitigation that resolved it in practice being a TЁПЛАЯ СТЕНА, «и всё МОМЕНТАЛЬНО ВЫСЫХАЛО», which is a direct dissent from this vault's "always required" position and is recorded as a Perspectives split**; **⚠️ the cat tray moved from the kitchen to the bathroom after «столько ХЕЙТА», turned to face the wall, and the remaining wish being a vanity height that would have concealed it**; **⚠️ and the ONE item still unsolved after a year — a towel point reachable FROM INSIDE the bath, distinct from the heated-rail position and evidently hard to retrofit.** [[_Sources/YT_lhikl-7c43c_nsdsgn_own_flat_year_one_fixes|note]].

- `yt_AH1INy0i5lU` — «Шумоизоляция квартиры — максимальная, на флагманском объекте» (2025-03-21, 34 min) — **FULL (fact_yield 33), `promotional_ratio: MEDIUM` (a named material system throughout — Premium М1 — and a named product, Техносонус В100; no discount code or funnel, but the material selection is not independent of the contractor's supply relationship), `corroborates_existing: partial`. Manual ru track. SPb stated, Петровская коса object. Guest: Руслан, «руководитель строительной организации» («срус»), генподрядчик.** **⚠️⚠️ SELECTED AS A DELIBERATE DEDUP TEST AGAINST THE VAULT'S DENSEST SUBJECT — soundproofing returns 200+ files — AND IT PASSED, NOT MARGINALLY. Two of its contributions are structural rather than incremental: (1) the ROOM-IN-A-ROOM principle presented as the ANSWER TO THE COMMONEST CLIENT REQUEST («эту стену обязательно шумоизолировать»), with the reason that request cannot work — «в основном всё это идёт через ПЕРЕКРЫТИЕ… по железобетонным конструкциям попадают уже к нам в квартиру»; and (2) SOCKETS AS THE DOMINANT ACOUSTIC LEAK, which this vault did not hold anywhere — zero prior mentions across all of `12_Engineering_and_Systems`, and its own gap-proportionality note lists door gaps, pipe penetrations and vent shafts but not sockets.** **⚠️ IT ALSO RESOLVED A ROUND 3 IDENTITY QUESTION: this is the Петровская коса object Round 1's Руслан named, so the Round 1 and Round 4 practitioners are CONFIRMED the same person by object — see the Round 3 caveat above, amended in place.** Highlights: **⚠️ the snowboard-jacket analogy, the clearest statement of the penetration problem in this vault — a waterproof membrane defeated by a zip and punched holes — with «многие зашивают стены акустическим гипсокартоном, делают виброподвесы, а потом просто ДЫРЯВЯТ эти стены»**; **⚠️⚠️ THE ШУМОБОКС AND ITS DESIGN-STAGE CONSTRAINT: «НЕ СТЫКОВАТЬ РОЗЕТКИ ДРУГ НАПРОТИВ ДРУГА, потому что иначе шумобоксы СТОЛКНУТСЯ и не хватит места просто для них» — so back-to-back sockets in adjacent rooms either cannot be executed or force the wall thicker, and nothing catches it because socket plans are drawn room by room**; **⚠️⚠️ A MEASURED WINDOW CASE THAT INVERTS A PURCHASING DECISION — a сталинка whose quiet DOUBLE-FRAME wooden windows were replaced with TRIPLE-GLAZED plastic and got LOUDER, confirmed with a sound meter, diagnosed as «ПЕНА НЕ ЯВЛЯЕТСЯ ЗВУКОИЗОЛИРУЮЩИМ МАТЕРИАЛОМ». This qualifies all four of this vault's quantified window levers at once: every one of them is defeated by a foam-filled perimeter, and the old window's two frames with a wide gap between them WERE the largest of those levers, delivered by construction**; **⚠️ the three noise types corroborated with two concrete additions — a LIFT SHAFT as a continuous structural source, and sockets as an airborne path**; **⚠️ the floor-versus-ceiling asymmetry corroborated with the labour reason stated plainly, AND a bound on what floor treatment buys (the impact path, not the structural one)**; **⚠️ a floor-first Premium М1 sequence with services integrated after the acoustic layers and a third blanket layer TURNED UP THE WALL, plus partition frames decoupled from the screed by a dedicated pad (Техносонус В100) under the guide profile rather than by tape alone**; **⚠️ a disclosed failure — «мы демонтировали ВСЮ СТЯЖКУ ЗА СВОЙ СЧЁТ [и] заново её перезалили»**; **⚠️ an 83.5 mm party-wall build-up, usefully thinner than this vault's ≈10 cm ASTic assembly, layer by layer, with «чем БОЛЬШЕ расстояние воздушное, тем ТИШЕ» as a monotonic rule**; **⚠️ A DIY FAILURE DIAGNOSED AS TWO DISTINCT ERRORS — пенопласт is THERMAL insulation not acoustic («поэтому у вас и не получилось»), and frameless systems require the substrate to be LEVELLED first with all joints sealed, which also explains the second complaint of preparing crooked panels for wallpaper**; **⚠️ a specific сталинка ceiling answer that declines the cheap options — framed system, PREMIUM grade minimum, bonded membrane, «а всё остальное — это просто ВЫКИНУТЬ ДЕНЬГИ» — recorded with the scope difference against this vault's opposing Ontario practice rather than as a flat contradiction**; **⚠️ a structural limitation of the whole product category (manufacturers' standards are written for COMMERCIAL rooms — studios, cinemas — so ceiling-track boxes do not exist and are sawn and deepened on site) yielding a CHECKABLE COMPETENCE SIGNAL: does the contractor have the manufacturer's technologists on call for non-standard junctions?**; **⚠️⚠️ AND THE SHARPEST QUESTION IN THE SOURCE, from a subscriber: why do soundproofing companies never measure before and after? «Менделеев писал: наука начинается с тех пор, как начинают ИЗМЕРЯТЬ. Получается, звукоизоляция — это РЕЛИГИЯ… чем больше за неё заплатил, тем сильнее ВЕРА, что она работает.» The contractor's answer — only laboratory-tested materials are used — is legitimate on materials SELECTION and does not address on-site verification, and the gap is recorded rather than let stand, because the window case earlier in the same video demonstrates it concretely: a certified unit performed worse than what it replaced. Material certification and installed performance are different quantities, and the second is what the client is buying. An unrelated channel on this vault reaches the same conclusion from the installation-quality side.** [[_Sources/YT_AH1INy0i5lU_nsdsgn_soundproofing_premium_object|note]].

The lesson: a new discipline predicts a new PERSPECTIVE, not a specific SUBJECT.** Highlights: **⚠️⚠️ THE COMMISSION-BIAS DISCLOSURE, unprompted and against his own interest — «ЧЕМ ХУЖЕ ЗАСТРОЙЩИК, ТЕМ ОН БОЛЬШЕ ПЛАТИТ — ТАК УСТРОЕН РЫНОК», with a market rate of 3-4% from developers, 1% on end-of-stock, and an explicit account of the distortion: «можно НАТЯГИВАТЬ СОВУ НА ГЛОБУС — создавать истории, в которых человек ХОЧЕТ этот дом… а порой риэлторы позволяют себе ГАДОСТИ говорить в адрес застройщика и УВОДИТЬ НА ТЕ ОБЪЕКТЫ, КТО ПЛАТИТ БОЛЬШЕ.» → A reading rule for any agent-sourced recommendation, structurally identical to this store's 2-15% designer procurement-commission range: the adviser is paid by the party whose product is recommended, and the payment is INVERSELY correlated with quality**; **⚠️⚠️ THE DESIGN FEE IS ROUGHLY SEGMENT-INDEPENDENT WHILE THE BUILD SCALES — «двушка в Мурино и супер-квартира на Петроградке: САМА ВОТ ЭТА ПАПОЧКА будет ПРИМЕРНО ОДИНАКОВАЯ… а дорогой ремонт от дешёвого будет отличаться МАТЕРИАЛАМИ» → the consequence neither speaker draws is that a percentage-of-budget fee charges a budget client the most in real terms and a premium client the least, which inverts how percentage pricing is usually justified**; **⚠️ why some designers cost more, where the substantive reason is an OPPORTUNITY-COST argument — «услуга одного окна… заказчик потратит больше, он МЕНЬШЕ ЗАРАБОТАЕТ, пока едет в магазин; ЭКОНОМИЯ ВРЕМЕНИ — ЭТО ТОЖЕ СЕРВИС» → which supplies the missing decision rule for whether the procurement commission is worth paying**; **⚠️⚠️ ABANDONMENT AFTER THE PROCUREMENT PHASE, a failure mode the commission structure CAUSES — a very expensive house past Vyborg where «дизайнеры ВСЁ ПРОДАЛИ, всё самое дорогое, а нужно же ещё мебель, немножко доделать — им стало НЕИНТЕРЕСНО, они просто ИСЧЕЗЛИ», and clients arriving «просто ПЕРЕПУГАННЫЕ… приходится их ЗАНОВО УЧИТЬ ДОВЕРЯТЬ ЛЮДЯМ». The money is front-loaded into buying, so the incentive is weakest when the remaining work is fiddliest → yields a specific vetting question: how is the post-procurement stage paid for?**; **⚠️ designers-as-snobs answered self-critically, with «надо НЕ ПУТАТЬ СЕБЯ — несчастного дизайнера — И КАКИХ-ТО КРУТЫХ ЛЮДЕЙ, НА КОТОРЫХ ТЫ РАБОТАЕШЬ, ПОТОМУ ЧТО ТЫ НЕ ОНИ», and a Moscow market where «дизайнеры реально ХАМЯТ: ну у вас дом ВСЕГО 200 м, о чём с вами говорить»**; **⚠️ what an agent actually does — «90% ВРЕМЕНИ мы РАЗГОВАРИВАЕМ С ЛЮДЬМИ… стараемся ПОМИРИТЬ внутренние потребности мужа [и] жены» — functionally the same elicitation-and-reconciliation work this vault records on the design side, from an unrelated profession**; **⚠️ the USABLE-AREA claim, ~50-60% полезной площади on a developer's 100 m² plan rising to «70, 72 в идеале» after replanning, asserted by a commercially adverse witness — ⚠️ and directly counterweighted by the corridor-absorption warning in the same round, which together establish that "usable area" is not a scalar**; **⚠️⚠️ УЮТ DEFINED WITH A MECHANISM, giving this vault a third hotel-vs-home argument and the most operational one: «уют — это когда есть ПРЕДМЕТЫ С ИСТОРИЕЙ… почему все говорят "это отель, нам не уютно"? …как будто ДУШИ НЕТ. Уют — это ты и твоя семья, где есть СЛЕДЫ СУЩЕСТВОВАНИЯ этой семьи; и ДО ТЕХ ПОР, ПОКА ЭТИХ СЛЕДОВ НЕТ, ЭТО БУДЕТ ОТЕЛЕМ» → cosiness cannot be specified at handover, only PROVIDED FOR; plus the surprising finding that for many clients уют IS clutter, so the word needs unpacking before it is designed to**; **⚠️ the same принцип via luggage — «почему Луи Виттон так дорого стоит — потому что это уже ИСТОРИЯ… рюкзак лет 10 ношу» — which is Round 4's благородное старение argument surviving a change of speaker and of object**; **⚠️ a design-project argument from someone with nothing to gain from it, with the two-component split (VISUAL, where he CONCEDES client taste — «не быть ДАЛЬТОНИКОМ — это не что-то выдающееся» — versus SCOPE DOCUMENTATION, which taste does not supply)**; **⚠️⚠️ «НЕТ НИЧЕГО БОЛЕЕ ПОСТОЯННОГО, ЧЕМ ВРЕМЕННОЕ» applied to whole flats — «люди покупают квартиры с какими-то ремонтами: "потом его сделаю" — в итоге НИКОГДА НИЧЕГО НЕ ДЕЛАЮТ, их всё в натуре БЕСИТ» → Round 4's temporary-furniture finding two orders of magnitude larger and from a different profession, with the same component-versus-substitute discriminator applying unchanged**; and **⚠️ a genuinely clever due-diligence technique whose PRINCIPLE is jurisdiction-independent: ask an insurer for a title-insurance quote and let their underwriting check the property for you — «они, ПРЕЖДЕ ЧЕМ тебе его сделать, ПРОВЕРЯЮТ твою квартиру… "благодарю, спасибо" — КВАРТИРА ЧИСТАЯ»; the QUOTE is the signal and you need not buy the policy.** ⚠️ **Russian market and finance throughout — the instruments (титульное страхование, справка формы 12, семейная ипотека) do not transfer to Belarus, and this store already treats a Russian advocate's purchase-risk source as comparative background only.** [[_Sources/YT_vTgY6vioNik_nsdsgn_realtor_vs_designers_podcast|note]].

and **an unsolved oven-glove problem he puts to the audience, recorded as an open gap.** ⚠️ Also the round's strongest methodological line: he **deliberately delayed reviewing his own flat** because «ОДНО ДЕЛО КРАСИВЫЕ КАРТИНКИ, А ДРУГОЕ ДЕЛО КАК ЭТО ВСЁ РАБОТАЕТ В ЖИЗНИ» — and he states the occupancy duration («больше месяца») himself, which is the honest caveat on the whole source.

NINE St Petersburg suppliers, each named, each closing with a pitch; structurally an industry showcase, usable because the nine accounts are mutually checkable.** **⚠️ MY SELECTION HYPOTHESIS WAS WRONG AND IS RECORDED AS SUCH: I picked it expecting ABSOLUTE price figures behind Round 5's "~2×" ratio, and there are almost none. It is a PERCENTAGES-AND-SUPPLY source — which is more useful for the vault than a 2022 price list would have been, but the prediction was still wrong, and the pattern is that I inferred content from a title containing «Цены».** **⚠️ No USD equivalents were produced, deliberately: a trailing 6-month USD/RUB mean before 2022-04-06 straddles the Feb–Mar 2022 shock and averages two incompatible regimes, so converting would manufacture false comparability — the vault's own price rule forbids it.** Content: **the DISPERSION is the finding — +20% to +150% across one market in one week, tracking import share and currency exposure** (Europe +25–50%; RU/BY ~+20% and then HELD; **hardware alone +100–150% because it is the one category with NO domestic substitute**, Blum cutting supply 60–70%; tile/sanitaryware +60–100%); **substitution HOLDS for structure and FAILS for finish** («Европа отличается более интересными отделками… и в России пока никто повторить их не может»), with decorative lighting «практически не развит» domestically; **LOGISTICS not production as the binding constraint**; **⚠️⚠️ panic buying documented to have locked in the WORST price of the period, with the second-order cost that it also produces the WRONG materials**; and three durable procurement techniques — **the MOVE-IN-HORIZON test (3–4 months → buy now; 2 years → no reason to hurry)**, **price-fixing by early purchase with FREE FACTORY STORAGE**, and **collapsing procurement into a single few-day window**, whose unstated precondition (whole spec complete and whole budget liquid on one day) I flag as mine. ⚠️ One claim flagged as a **sales pitch for his own service**: design fees repricing more slowly than materials — routed with the flag, because the structural half (fees are sticky in TIME as well as in SEGMENT) is real. **⚠️ Also supplies a THIRD ASR rendering of the host's surname («Сенчагов»), which retrospectively strengthens the Round 5 correction — see `Change_Log.md`.**
